    }
    const canvas = (w, h) => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; return c; };

    async function make(cut, k, z) {
      const lw = OL.line / z, bl = OL.blur / z, m = lw + bl * 1.5 + 2;
      const w0 = cut.bb[2] - cut.bb[0] + m * 2, h0 = cut.bb[3] - cut.bb[1] + m * 2;
      k = Math.min(k, OL.max / w0, OL.max / h0);
      const x = Math.floor((cut.bb[0] - m) * k) / k, y = Math.floor((cut.bb[1] - m) * k) / k;
      const r = await raster(cut.defs + cut.xml, x, y, w0 + 1 / k, h0 + 1 / k, k, 0);
      return glow({ c: r.c, x, y, k }, z);
    }

    function glow(mk, z) {
      const t0 = performance.now(), k = mk.k, x = mk.x, y = mk.y;
      const lw = OL.line / z, bl = OL.blur / z;
      const mask = mk.c, W = mask.width, H = mask.height, L = lw * k;

      const ring = canvas(W, H), g = ring.getContext('2d');
      const n = Math.max(12, Math.min(32, Math.ceil(L * 5)));
      for (const rr of [L, L * 0.55]) for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = (i / n) * Math.PI * 2; g.drawImage(mask, Math.cos(a) * rr, Math.sin(a) * rr); }
      g.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-in'; g.fillStyle = OL.color; g.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      g.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out'; g.drawImage(mask, 0, 0);

      const out = canvas(W, H), q = out.getContext('2d');
      q.shadowColor = OL.glow; q.shadowBlur = bl * k; q.drawImage(ring, 0, 0); q.shadowBlur = bl * k * 0.45; q.drawImage(ring, 0, 0);
      q.shadowBlur = 0; q.shadowColor = 'transparent';
      q.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out'; q.globalAlpha = 0.6; q.drawImage(mask, 0, 0);
      q.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over'; q.globalAlpha = 1; q.drawImage(ring, 0, 0);
      mask.width = mask.height = 0; ring.width = ring.height = 0;
      st.bakes++; st.bakeMs += performance.now() - t0; st.px = Math.max(st.px, W * H);
      return { c: out, x, y, k, z, tex: null, on: 0 };
    }
    return {
      prep,
      stats: () => ({ ...st, bakeMs: Math.round(st.bakeMs), ids: cuts ? Object.keys(cuts) : null, cached: cache.size }),

      bake(id, k, z) {

        const BKo = window.LGCityBake && window.LGCityBake.olMask ? window.LGCityBake.olMask(sources, id, k) : null;
        const cut = BKo ? null : prep(true)[id]; if (!BKo && !cut) return Promise.resolve(null);
        const key = id + '|' + k.toFixed(3);
        let e = cache.get(key);

        if (!e) for (const [k2, e2] of cache) if (k2.startsWith(id + '|') && e2 && typeof e2.then !== 'function' && e2.kq && fits(e2.kq, k) && e2.z && e2.z <= z * 1.2 && e2.z * 1.2 >= z) { cache.delete(k2); cache.set(k2, e2); return Promise.resolve(e2); }
        if (e) { cache.delete(key); cache.set(key, e); return Promise.resolve(e); }
        e = (BKo ? BKo.then((mk) => { if (mk) return glow(mk, z); const c2 = prep(true)[id]; return c2 ? make(c2, k, z) : null; }) : make(cut, k, z)).then((r) => { if (r) r.kq = k; cache.set(key, r); trim(); return r; }, (err) => { cache.delete(key); console.warn('[город] обводка', id, err); return null; });
        cache.set(key, e);
        return e;
      },


      shape(id, xml, bb, k, z) {
        const key = '~' + id + '|' + k.toFixed(3);
        let e = cache.get(key);
        if (e) { cache.delete(key); cache.set(key, e); return Promise.resolve(e); }
        e = make({ xml, defs: '', bb }, k, z).then((r) => { if (r) r.kq = k; cache.set(key, r); trim(); return r; }, (err) => { cache.delete(key); console.warn('[город] обводка', id, err); return null; });
        cache.set(key, e);
        return e;
      },
      clear,

      set(tag, list) { for (let i = sources.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (sources[i] && sources[i].tag === tag) sources.splice(i, 1); for (const l of list || []) sources.push({ ...l, tag }); cuts = null;
        for (const [key, e] of cache) if (e && typeof e.then !== 'function' && !(e.on > 0)) { if (e.tex) try { e.tex.destroy(true); } catch {} cache.delete(key); } },
    };
    function clear() { for (const e of cache.values()) if (e && e.tex) try { e.tex.destroy(true); } catch {} cache.clear(); }

    function trim() {
      for (const [key, e] of cache) {
        if (cache.size <= OL.keep) break;
        if (!e || typeof e.then === 'function' || e.on > 0) continue;
        if (e.tex) try { e.tex.destroy(true); } catch {}
        e.c.width = e.c.height = 0; cache.delete(key);
      }
    }
  }




  const extOf = (o) => (o.ext ? o.ext : [0, 0, o.W, o.H]);
  const BAKE_IN = { raster, measure, deadPaint, OL };

   


  function create(o) {
    const P = window.PIXI;
    const G = { mode: '', ok: false, dead: false };



    const dpr = () => Math.min(4, (window.devicePixelRatio || 1) * (o.resK && G.mode !== 'canvas' ? Math.max(1, +o.resK() || 1) : 1));

    G.qcap = 4;
    const res = () => Math.min(dpr(), G.qcap, G.mode === 'canvas' ? CANVAS_RES : 4);
    const reres = () => { if (renderer && renderer.resolution !== res()) { renderer.resize(Math.max(1, cam.vw), Math.max(1, cam.vh), res()); G.view(cam.x, cam.y, cam.z, cam.vw, cam.vh, true); } };
    let renderer = null, canvas = null, dirty = true;
    let cam = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0.6, vw: 360, vh: 400 }, k = 1, kT = 1, gen = 0, TS = TILE;
    const stage = new P.Container(), world = new P.Container(), bg = new P.Container(), walk = new P.Container(), names = new P.Container();
    const hl = new P.Container();
    const bgD = new P.Container();
    walk.sortableChildren = true; names.sortableChildren = true;
    world.addChild(bg, bgD, hl, walk, names); stage.addChild(world);
    const lst = labelStyle(o.labelHost || o.host); lst.k = +o.labK || 1;
    const need = () => { dirty = true; if (o.onDirty) o.onDirty(); };



    const layers = o.layers.map((l) => { const m = measure(l.svg, false); return { xml: m.xml, x: l.x, y: l.y, w: l.w, h: l.h, dxml: l.dead ? measure(l.dead, false).xml : '', npc: !l.dead && m.xml.includes('cy-npcg') ? npcBoxes(m.xml) : [] }; });
    const npcs = layers.flatMap((l) => l.npc);
    const sky = skyDef(o.sky, o.H);
    const skyD = skyDef(o.skyDead || 'linear-gradient(#05060a 0%, #0b0d14 30%, #151824 58%, #1f2330 100%)', o.H);
    let mort = false, wave = null, maskG = null;

    const BK = bakeOf(o, P, need, { dart: () => deadArt });

    const kill = (s) => { if (s) s.destroy(!s.__bk); };

     
    const Q = []; let busy = false;
    function job(j) { Q.push(j); pump(); }
    async function pump() {
      if (busy) return; busy = true;
      try {


        while (Q.length && !G.dead && !G.zooming) {
          Q.sort((a, b) => a.pri() - b.pri());
          const j = Q.shift();
          if (j.skip && j.skip()) continue;
          try { await j.run(); } catch (e) { console.warn('[город] не запеклось', e); }
        }
      } finally { busy = false; }
    }

     
    const tiles = new Map();
    let old = [];


    let deadArt = { under: '', over: '' };
    function tileXml(x, y, s, dd, raw) {
      const hit = (l) => l.x < x + s && l.x + l.w > x && l.y < y + s && l.y + l.h > y;


      const nh = (l) => l.npc.some((b) => b.x < x + s && b.x + b.w > x && b.y < y + s && b.y + b.h > y);
      if (raw) {
        let out = ''; for (const l of layers) if (l.dxml && hit(l)) out += l.dxml;
        let gh = ''; for (const l of layers) if (!l.dxml && l.npc.length && nh(l)) gh += `<g class="cy-gho">${l.xml}</g>`;
        if (gh) out += '<style>.cy-gho *{visibility:hidden}.cy-gho .cy-npcg,.cy-gho .cy-npcg *{visibility:visible}.cy-gho .cy-npcg{display:inline}</style>' + gh;
        return out + (deadArt.over || '');
      }
      let out = `<defs>${dd ? skyD : sky}</defs><rect x="${x - 2}" y="${y - 2}" width="${s + 4}" height="${s + 4}" fill="url(#cyglsky)"/>${dd && npcs.length ? '<style>.cy-npc{display:none}</style>' : ''}`;
      for (const l of layers) if (hit(l) && !(dd && l.dxml)) out += l.xml;
      return out + (dd ? deadArt.under || '' : '');
    }
    function wantTiles(vx, vy, vw, vh, md = '', now = performance.now()) {
      const dd = md === 'd', cont = dd ? bgD : bg;
      const bm = BK ? BK.mode(md) : 'off';


      if (bm === 'on') return BK.tiles(cont, vx, vy, vw, vh, md, Math.min(kT, Math.max(0.3, cam.z * res())), now);
      if (bm === 'wait') return 1;

      const [X0, Y0, X1, Y1] = extOf(o);
      const i0 = Math.max(Math.floor(X0 / TS), Math.floor((vx - TS * 0.25) / TS)), i1 = Math.min(Math.ceil(X1 / TS) - 1, Math.floor((vx + vw + TS * 0.25) / TS));
      const j0 = Math.max(Math.floor(Y0 / TS), Math.floor((Math.max(o.ext ? Y0 : o.top || 0, vy) - TS * 0.25) / TS)), j1 = Math.min(Math.ceil(Y1 / TS) - 1, Math.floor((vy + vh + TS * 0.25) / TS));
      const g0 = gen, kk = k, kq = k.toFixed(4);
      let missing = 0;
      for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) {
        const key = kq + '|' + i + ',' + j + md; let t = tiles.get(key);
        const inView = (i + 1) * TS > vx && i * TS < vx + vw && (j + 1) * TS > vy && j * TS < vy + vh;
        if (t) { t.used = now; if (t.spr) t.spr.visible = true; if (!t.spr && inView) missing++; continue; }
        if (inView) missing++;
        t = { gen: g0, used: now, spr: null, md, k: kk };
        tiles.set(key, t);
        const x = i * TS, y = j * TS, s = TS;

        const gone = () => G.dead || kk !== k || tiles.get(key) !== t;
        job({
          pri: () => { const cx = cam.x + cam.vw / cam.z / 2, cy = cam.y + cam.vh / cam.z / 2; return (inView ? 0 : 5) + Math.hypot(x + s / 2 - cx, y + s / 2 - cy) / 1e5; },
          skip: () => { if (!gone()) return false; if (tiles.get(key) === t && !t.spr) tiles.delete(key); return true; },
          run: async () => {
            const r = await raster(tileXml(x, y, s, dd), x, y, s, s, kk, 1);
            if (gone()) { if (tiles.get(key) === t && !t.spr) tiles.delete(key); return; }
            if (dd) {
              deadPaint(r.c);
              const top = tileXml(x, y, s, true, true);
              if (top) { const r2 = await raster(top, x, y, s, s, kk, 1); if (gone()) { if (tiles.get(key) === t && !t.spr) tiles.delete(key); return; } r.c.getContext('2d').drawImage(r2.c, 0, 0); }
            }
            const spr = new P.Sprite(texOf(r)); spr.position.set(x, y); spr.scale.set(1 / kk);
            t.spr = spr; cont.addChild(spr);
            need();
          },
        });
      }


      for (const t of tiles.values()) if (t.spr && t.md === md && t.used < now && (t.k === kk || !missing || !t.spr.visible)) t.spr.visible = false;

      const pxPer = TILE * TILE;
      if (tiles.size * pxPer > TILE_BUDGET) {
        const far = [...tiles.entries()].filter(([, t]) => t.used < now && t.spr && !t.spr.visible).sort((a, b) => a[1].used - b[1].used);
        while (tiles.size * pxPer > TILE_BUDGET && far.length) { const [key, t] = far.shift(); t.spr.destroy(true); tiles.delete(key); }
      }
      return missing;
    }

     
    const props = o.props.map((p) => ({ ...p, spr: null, gen: -1, q: false, box: [p.x + (p.dx || 0) - 160, p.y - 160, p.w + 320, p.h + 320] }));

    const FLK = [[0, 1, 1, 1], [0.3, 0.94, 1.07, 0.92], [0.55, 1.04, 0.95, 1], [0.8, 0.97, 1.04, 0.95], [1, 1, 1, 1]];
    const flickAt = (t) => { const u = (t / 1700) % 1; let i = 1; while (i < FLK.length - 1 && u > FLK[i][0]) i++; const a = FLK[i - 1], b = FLK[i], v = (u - a[0]) / (b[0] - a[0]), e = v * v * (3 - 2 * v); return [a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * e, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * e, a[3] + (b[3] - a[3]) * e]; };
    const waveR = () => (wave ? Math.max(0, (performance.now() - wave.t0) / wave.ms) * wave.R : Infinity);
    function wantProps(vx, vy, vw, vh) {
      const wr = waveR();
      for (const p of props) {
        const [bx, by, bw, bh] = p.box, on = bx < vx + vw && bx + bw > vx && by < vy + vh && by + bh > vy;

        const wd = mort && !p.tag, dOn = wd && !!p.sprD && Math.hypot(p.box[0] + p.box[2] / 2 - (wave ? wave.x : 0), p.box[1] + p.box[3] - (wave ? wave.y : 0)) <= wr;
        if (p.sprD) p.sprD.visible = on && dOn;
        if (p.spr) p.spr.visible = on && !dOn;
        if (on && wd && p.genD !== gen && !p.qD) { if (p.sprD && fits(p.kD, k)) p.genD = gen; else bakeDead(p); }
        if (!on || p.gen === gen || p.q) continue;
        if (p.spr && fits(p.k, k)) { p.gen = gen; continue; }
        p.q = true; const g0 = gen, kk = k;
        job({
          pri: () => 1, skip: () => { if (G.dead || g0 !== gen || p.dead) { p.q = false; return true; } return false; },
          run: async () => {
            p.q = false;

            const bt = BK && !(p.paint && mort) ? await BK.prop(p, kk, '') : null;
            if (G.dead || g0 !== gen || p.dead) return;
            if (bt && p.spr && p.spr.texture === bt.tex) { p.gen = g0; p.k = bt.k; return; }
            let tex, x0, y0, kp = kk, cw, ch, rr = null;
            if (bt) ({ tex, x0, y0, k: kp, cw, ch } = bt);
            else {
              if (!p.m) p.m = measure(p.svg, true);
              const b = p.m.bb || { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: p.w, h: p.h };

              x0 = Math.floor((b.x - 8 + (p.dx || 0)) * kk) / kk - (p.dx || 0); y0 = Math.floor((b.y - 8) * kk) / kk;
              const r = await raster(p.m.xml, x0, y0, b.x + b.w + 8 - x0, b.y + b.h + 8 - y0, kk, 1);
              if (G.dead || g0 !== gen || p.dead) return;
              if (p.paint && mort) deadPaint(r.c);
              tex = texOf(r); cw = r.cw; ch = r.ch; rr = r;
            }
            const spr = new P.Sprite(tex); spr.position.set(x0 + (p.dx || 0), y0); spr.scale.set(1 / kp); spr.zIndex = p.z;
            if (bt) spr.__bk = 1;
            if (G.ptint != null && !p.tag) spr.tint = G.ptint;
            if (p.spin) { spr.pivot.set((p.spin[0] - x0) * kp, (p.spin[1] - y0) * kp); spr.position.set(p.spin[0] + (p.dx || 0), p.spin[1]); }
            if (p.flick) { spr.pivot.set((p.flick[0] - x0) * kp, (p.flick[1] - y0) * kp); spr.position.set(p.flick[0] + (p.dx || 0), p.flick[1]); spr.__k = kp; }
            kill(p.spr);
            p.spr = spr; p.gen = g0; p.k = kp; p.box = [x0 + (p.dx || 0), y0, cw / kp, ch / kp];


            p.mask = p.spin || p.flick || p.pulse ? null : p.mask || maskOf(bt ? { c: bt.sh.img, sx: bt.fr[1], sy: bt.fr[2], cw, ch } : rr, kp);
            seeLast = -1;
            walk.addChild(spr);
            need();
          },
        });
      }
    }

    function bakeDead(p) {
      p.qD = true; const g0 = gen, kk = k;
      job({
        pri: () => 1.5, skip: () => { if (G.dead || g0 !== gen || p.dead || !mort) { p.qD = false; return true; } return false; },
        run: async () => {
          p.qD = false;

          const bt = BK ? await BK.prop(p, kk, 'd') : null;
          if (G.dead || g0 !== gen || p.dead || !mort) return;
          const spr = bt ? Object.assign(new P.Sprite(bt.tex), { __bk: 1 }) : await (async () => {

            const own = !!p.svgDead; if (own && !p.md) p.md = measure(p.svgDead, true); if (!p.m) p.m = measure(p.svg, true);
            const m = own ? p.md : p.m, b = m.bb || { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: p.w, h: p.h };
            const x0 = Math.floor((b.x - 8 + (p.dx || 0)) * kk) / kk - (p.dx || 0), y0 = Math.floor((b.y - 8) * kk) / kk;
            const r = await raster(m.xml, x0, y0, b.x + b.w + 8 - x0, b.y + b.h + 8 - y0, kk, 1);
            if (G.dead || g0 !== gen || p.dead || !mort) return null;
            if (!own) deadPaint(r.c);
            const spr = new P.Sprite(texOf(r)); spr.position.set(x0 + (p.dx || 0), y0); spr.scale.set(1 / kk);
            return spr;
          })();
          if (!spr) return;
          if (bt) { spr.position.set(bt.x0 + (p.dx || 0), bt.y0); spr.scale.set(1 / bt.k); }
          spr.zIndex = p.z; spr.visible = false;
          kill(p.sprD);
          p.sprD = spr; p.genD = g0; p.kD = bt ? bt.k : kk; walk.addChild(spr); need();
        },
      });
    }

     
    const looks = new Map();
    



    const LOOK_LV = [0.5, 0.71, 1, 1.41, 2, K_MAX];
    const lookLv = (kn) => { for (const L of LOOK_LV) if (L * KUP >= kn) return L; return K_MAX; };
    const lookSeen = (svg) => { for (const w of walkers) if (w.gl && w.gl.svg === svg && w.gl.root.visible && !w.cull) return true; return false; };
    function look(svg) {
      let L = looks.get(svg);
      if (!L) { L = { tex: null, refs: 0, gen: -1, p: null, bb: null, used: 0 }; looks.set(svg, L); }
      return L;
    }
    function bakeLook(svg, L) {
      if (L.p || (o.lookK ? !!L.tex : L.gen === gen)) return;
      const kk = +o.lookK || lookLv(k);
      if (!o.lookK && L.tex && L.bb && L.bb.k === kk) { L.gen = gen; return; }
      const ai = !o.lookK && L.alts ? L.alts.findIndex((a) => a.bb.k === kk) : -1;
      if (ai >= 0) {
        const a = L.alts.splice(ai, 1)[0]; if (L.tex) L.alts.unshift({ tex: L.tex, bb: L.bb }); L.tex = a.tex; L.bb = a.bb; L.gen = gen;
        for (const w of walkers) if (w.gl && w.gl.svg === svg) skin(w);
        need(); return;
      }
      const g0 = gen;
      L.p = true;
      job({
        pri: () => (lookSeen(svg) ? 0.5 : 6), skip: () => { if (G.dead || !L.refs) { L.p = null; return true; } return false; },
        run: async () => {

          if (!L.m) L.m = measure(svg.replace(/<svg\b/, '<svg x="0" y="0" width="120" height="170" overflow="visible"'), true, true);
          const m = L.m, b = m.bb || { x: 0, y: 0, w: 120, h: 170 };
          const x0 = b.x - 10, y0 = b.y - 10;
          const r = await raster(m.xml, x0, y0, b.w + 20, b.h + 20, kk, 1);
          L.p = null;
          if (G.dead) return;
          const t = texOf(r);
          if (L.tex && !o.lookK) { L.alts = L.alts || []; L.alts.unshift({ tex: L.tex, bb: L.bb }); for (const a of L.alts.splice(2)) a.tex.destroy(true); }
          else if (L.tex) L.tex.destroy(true);
          L.tex = t; L.gen = g0; L.bb = { x: x0, y: y0, k: kk };
          for (const w of walkers) if (w.gl && w.gl.svg === svg) skin(w);
          if (g0 !== gen && L.refs && !o.lookK) bakeLook(svg, L);
          need();
        },
      });
    }
    function skin(w) {
      const L = looks.get(w.gl.svg); const s = w.gl.spr;
      if (!L || !L.tex) { s.visible = false; return; }
      s.texture = L.tex; s.visible = true;
      s.position.set(L.bb.x - 60, L.bb.y - 170); s.scale.set(1 / L.bb.k);
    }
    function release(svg) {
      const L = looks.get(svg); if (!L) return;
      L.refs--; L.used = performance.now();

      const idle = [...looks.entries()].filter(([, x]) => x.refs <= 0);
      if (idle.length > 40) { idle.sort((a, b) => a[1].used - b[1].used); for (const [key, x] of idle.slice(0, idle.length - 40)) { if (x.tex) x.tex.destroy(true); if (x.alts) for (const a of x.alts) a.tex.destroy(true); looks.delete(key); } }
    }
    const walkers = new Set();
    function labelTex(w) {
      const d = res(), r = labelCanvas(w.gl.lab, lst, d);
      const t = new P.Texture({ source: new P.CanvasSource({ resource: r.c }) });
      if (w.gl.nm.texture && w.gl.nm.texture !== P.Texture.EMPTY) w.gl.nm.texture.destroy(true);
      w.gl.nm.texture = t; w.gl.lw = r.w; w.gl.lh = r.h; w.gl.ld = d;
    }

     
    const rips = [];

     
    const pref = o.mode === 'canvas' ? ['canvas'] : ['webgl', 'canvas'];
    const ready = (async () => {
      await loadFont(o.V);
      try { await Promise.race([document.fonts && document.fonts.ready, new Promise((ok) => setTimeout(ok, 1500))]); } catch {}
      const make = (preference) => P.autoDetectRenderer({
        preference, failIfMajorPerformanceCaveat: o.mode !== 'webgl',
        width: Math.max(1, cam.vw), height: Math.max(1, cam.vh), resolution: res(), autoDensity: true,
        background: '#0d0b22', antialias: false, powerPreference: 'low-power', clearBeforeRender: true,
        eventMode: 'none', eventFeatures: { move: false, globalMove: false, click: false, wheel: false },
      });
      renderer = await make(pref);

      if (renderer.name !== 'canvas' && o.mode !== 'webgl') {
        let soft = false;
        try { const gl = renderer.gl, ext = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info'); soft = /swiftshader|llvmpipe|softpipe|software|basic render/i.test(String(gl.getParameter(ext ? ext.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL : gl.RENDERER))); } catch {}
        if (soft) { G.soft = true; try { renderer.destroy(); } catch {} renderer = await make(['canvas']); }
      }
      if (G.dead) { renderer.destroy(); return; }


      try { for (const T of [P.Ticker.system, P.Ticker.shared]) { T.autoStart = false; T.stop(); } } catch {}
      G.mode = renderer.name === 'canvas' ? 'canvas' : 'webgl';
      reres();
      canvas = renderer.canvas;
      canvas.className = 'cy-gl';
      canvas.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      o.host.insertBefore(canvas, o.host.firstChild);

      canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', () => { clearTimeout(G.lostT); G.lostT = setTimeout(() => { if (!G.dead && o.onFail) o.onFail('WebGL потерян'); }, 3000); });
      canvas.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', () => { clearTimeout(G.lostT); need(); });
      G.ok = true;
      need();
    })().catch((e) => { console.warn('[город] Pixi не запустился — SVG', e); if (!G.dead && o.onFail) o.onFail(String(e && e.message || e)); });


    let slow = 0, frames = 0;
    G.ready = ready;
    G.view = (x, y, z, vw, vh, now) => {
      const resized = vw !== cam.vw || vh !== cam.vh;
      cam = { x, y, z, vw: vw || cam.vw, vh: vh || cam.vh };
      if (renderer && (resized || renderer.resolution !== res())) renderer.resize(cam.vw, cam.vh, res());



      const nk = Math.min(K_MAX, Math.max(0.3, z * res()));

      if ((Math.abs(nk / kT - 1) > 0.004 || gen === 0) && !(G.zooming && nk === G.nkP && !now)) {
        clearTimeout(G.rk); G.nkP = nk;
        const go = () => {
          kT = nk; G.zooming = false;
          if (gen === 0 || !fits(k, nk)) {
            let best = 0;
            for (const t of tiles.values()) if (t.spr && fits(t.k, nk) && (!best || t.k < best)) best = t.k;
            k = best || nk; gen++; TS = TILE / k;
            if (!o.lookK) for (const w of walkers) if (w.gl) bakeLook(w.gl.svg, look(w.gl.svg));
          }
          for (const w of walkers) if (w.gl && w.gl.ld !== res()) labelTex(w);
          need(); pump();
        };
        if (gen === 0 || now) go(); else { G.zooming = true; G.rk = setTimeout(go, 250); }
      } else if (G.zooming && Math.abs(nk / kT - 1) <= 0.004) { clearTimeout(G.rk); G.zooming = false; pump(); need(); }
      dirty = true;
    };
    G.add = (w, svg, lab) => {

    const anv = an ? { ox: Math.round((an.vis[0] + an.vis[2]) / 2 - 480), oy: Math.round(an.vis[3] - 66 - 330), k: 2 } : null;
    const hit = (b) => anv && b.x < anv.ox + 960 && b.x + b.w > anv.ox && b.y < anv.oy + 540 + 200 && b.y + b.h > anv.oy;


    return { map, layers: R.layers, props: R.props, sky: R.sky, anv, near: { layers: R.layers.filter(hit), props: R.props.filter(hit) }, scale: K.SCALE, spawn: spawn(T), id: L.id, biome: L.biome, fx: R.fx,
      ext: R.ext || null, edge: R.ext && L.edge ? { ...L.edge } : null, glade: R.glade || null };
  }
   
  function spawn(T) { const L = K.layoutOf(T), g = L.gate; return L.spawn ? L.spawn.slice() : g ? [g.door.x, g.door.y - 170] : [L.W / 2, L.H / 2]; }
  window.LGTownKitCity = { meta, buildings, wrap, build, spawn, SCALE: K.SCALE, SPEED: K.SPEED };
})();

===== END FILE /site/tk-city.js =====
